======================================================================
CLOUD COMPUTING KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Cloud Computing Fundamentals
Concept: Cloud Computing
Definition: Delivery of computing services over the Internet.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in cloud computing.
- Commonly used in enterprise cloud architectures.
- Frequently asked in cloud certification exams and interviews.
Example:
Cloud Computing is widely used in modern cloud environments.
Related Concepts:
Virtualization, Containers, Networking, Security, DevOps, Distributed Systems
